Design:
- First layer computes a few scalar features:
  - paddle position (scaled),
  - ball x (scaled),
  - horizontal ball direction from ball minus trail x,
  - three ball-row threshold features (y >= 4, y >= 6, y >= 8).
- Second layer forms right/left “lead” features:
  - right lead is active when dx = +1 and the ball is low enough,
  - left lead is active when dx = -1 and the ball is low enough,
  - the three row thresholds give a coarse lead of about 6, 3, or 0 cells depending on how far the ball is from the bottom.
- Output layer compares the predicted target column (ball x + lead) with the paddle column and strongly favors left/right; no-op is favored when the difference is zero.
